generator client {
  provider = "prisma-client-js"
}

datasource db {
  provider = "sqlite"
}

enum UserRole {
  ADMIN
  COUNSELOR
  SCHOOL_OWNER
  STAFF
}

enum ImportSessionStatus {
  DRAFT
  PARSED
  COMMITTED
  FAILED
  CANCELED
}

enum ImportRowStatus {
  PENDING
  VALID
  INVALID
  CREATED
  UPDATED
  SKIPPED
  CONFLICT
}

enum Gender {
  MALE
  FEMALE
  UNKNOWN
}

enum ServiceStatus {
  ACTIVE
  INACTIVE
  COMING_SOON
}

enum WorkflowStatus {
  DRAFT
  ACTIVE
  ARCHIVED
}

enum FieldType {
  TEXT
  TEXTAREA
  NUMBER
  DATE
  SELECT
  MULTI_SELECT
  CHECKBOX
  RADIO
  FILE_UPLOAD
  IMAGE_UPLOAD
  STUDENT_PICKER
  PARENT_PICKER
  STAFF_PICKER
  REPEATER
  SIGNATURE
  RICH_TEXT
}

enum CaseStatus {
  DRAFT
  SUBMITTED
  ARCHIVED
}

enum EvidenceType {
  IMAGE
  FILE
  LINK
}

enum SubscriptionStatus {
  TRIAL
  ACTIVE
  PAST_DUE
  CANCELED
  EXPIRED
}

enum PaymentStatus {
  PENDING
  PAID
  FAILED
  REFUNDED
  CANCELED
}

enum PaymentMethod {
  CARD
  BANK_TRANSFER
  MANUAL
}

enum IntegrationStatus {
  ACTIVE
  INACTIVE
  ERROR
}

model SchoolAccount {
  id                    String                 @id @default(cuid())
  name                  String
  slug                  String                 @unique
  isActive              Boolean                @default(true)
  studentImportSessions StudentImportSession[]

  users           User[]
  profile         SchoolProfile?
  students        Student[]
  guardians       Guardian[]
  staff           StaffMember[]
  services        ServiceAccess[]
  cases           CaseEntry[]
  subscription    Subscription?
  resultsAnalyses ResultsAnalysis[]
  createdAt       DateTime          @default(now())
  updatedAt       DateTime          @updatedAt
}

model User {
  id              String         @id @default(cuid())
  schoolAccountId String?
  schoolAccount   SchoolAccount? @relation(fields: [schoolAccountId], references: [id])

  name         String
  email        String   @unique
  phone        String?
  passwordHash String?
  role         UserRole @default(COUNSELOR)
  gender       Gender   @default(UNKNOWN)
  officialName String?
  jobTitle     String?
  isActive     Boolean  @default(true)

  onboardingCompleted   Boolean   @default(false)
  onboardingCompletedAt DateTime?
  onboardingSkippedAt   DateTime?

  cases    CaseEntry[]
  sessions UserSession[]

  createdAt              DateTime         @default(now())
  updatedAt              DateTime         @updatedAt
  caseEvidences          CaseEvidence[]
  createdReportTemplates ReportTemplate[]

  @@index([schoolAccountId])
}

model UserSession {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  userId String
  user   User   @relation(fields: [userId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  tokenId String @unique

  userAgent String?
  ipAddress String?

  isActive   Boolean   @default(true)
  expiresAt  DateTime
  lastSeenAt DateTime  @default(now())
  revokedAt  DateTime?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([userId])
  @@index([tokenId])
  @@index([isActive])
  @@index([expiresAt])
}

model SchoolProfile {
  id              String        @id @default(cuid())
  schoolAccountId String        @unique
  schoolAccount   SchoolAccount @relation(fields: [schoolAccountId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  schoolName          String
  principalName       String?
  educationDepartment String?
  educationOffice     String?
  city                String?
  district            String?
  stage               String?
  academicYear        String?
  currentSemester     String?
  logoUrl             String?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt
}

model Guardian {
  id              String        @id @default(cuid())
  schoolAccountId String
  schoolAccount   SchoolAccount @relation(fields: [schoolAccountId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  name     String
  phone    String?
  relation String?

  students Student[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([schoolAccountId])
}

model Student {
  id              String        @id @default(cuid())
  schoolAccountId String
  schoolAccount   SchoolAccount @relation(fields: [schoolAccountId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  guardianId String?
  guardian   Guardian? @relation(fields: [guardianId], references: [id])

  fullName   String
  nationalId String?
  gender     Gender  @default(UNKNOWN)
  stage      String?
  grade      String?
  classroom  String?
  phone      String?
  isActive   Boolean @default(true)

  cases CaseEntry[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([schoolAccountId])
  @@index([guardianId])
  @@index([fullName])
}

model StaffMember {
  id              String        @id @default(cuid())
  schoolAccountId String
  schoolAccount   SchoolAccount @relation(fields: [schoolAccountId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  name      String
  roleTitle String?
  phone     String?
  email     String?
  gender    Gender  @default(UNKNOWN)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([schoolAccountId])
}

model Service {
  id          String        @id @default(cuid())
  slug        String        @unique
  name        String
  description String?
  status      ServiceStatus @default(ACTIVE)

  workflows Workflow[]
  cases     CaseEntry[]
  access    ServiceAccess[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt
}

model Workflow {
  id        String  @id @default(cuid())
  serviceId String
  service   Service @relation(fields: [serviceId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  name         String
  workflowType String         @default("default")
  version      Int            @default(1)
  status       WorkflowStatus @default(DRAFT)
  isActive     Boolean        @default(false)

  steps WorkflowStep[]
  cases CaseEntry[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([serviceId])
  @@index([serviceId, workflowType, isActive])
}

model WorkflowStep {
  id         String   @id @default(cuid())
  workflowId String
  workflow   Workflow @relation(fields: [workflowId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  title       String
  description String?
  order       Int

  fields DynamicField[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([workflowId])
  @@index([order])
}

model DynamicField {
  id     String       @id @default(cuid())
  stepId String
  step   WorkflowStep @relation(fields: [stepId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  key         String
  label       String
  type        FieldType
  placeholder String?
  helpText    String?
  isRequired  Boolean   @default(false)
  order       Int

  dependsOnFieldKey String?
  linkedToValue     String?
  allowOther        Boolean @default(false)
  isRepeater        Boolean @default(false)

  options DynamicFieldOption[]
  values  CaseValue[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([stepId])
  @@index([key])
  @@index([order])
}

model DynamicFieldOption {
  id      String       @id @default(cuid())
  fieldId String
  field   DynamicField @relation(fields: [fieldId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  label         String
  value         String
  order         Int
  linkedToValue String?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([fieldId])
  @@index([value])
}

model CaseEntry {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  schoolAccountId String
  schoolAccount   SchoolAccount @relation(fields: [schoolAccountId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  serviceId String
  service   Service @relation(fields: [serviceId], references: [id])

  workflowId      String?
  workflow        Workflow?        @relation(fields: [workflowId], references: [id])
  guidanceReports GuidanceReport[]
  studentId       String?
  student         Student?         @relation(fields: [studentId], references: [id])

  createdById String?
  createdBy   User?   @relation(fields: [createdById], references: [id])

  title       String?
  status      CaseStatus @default(DRAFT)
  submittedAt DateTime?

  values    CaseValue[]
  evidences Evidence[]

  createdAt              DateTime         @default(now())
  updatedAt              DateTime         @updatedAt
  caseEvidences          CaseEvidence[]
  createdReportTemplates ReportTemplate[]

  @@index([schoolAccountId])
  @@index([serviceId])
  @@index([workflowId])
  @@index([studentId])
  @@index([status])
}

model CaseValue {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  caseEntryId String
  caseEntry   CaseEntry @relation(fields: [caseEntryId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  fieldId String?
  field   DynamicField? @relation(fields: [fieldId], references: [id])

  fieldKey  String
  value     String?
  jsonValue Json?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([caseEntryId])
  @@index([fieldKey])
}

model Evidence {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  caseEntryId String
  caseEntry   CaseEntry @relation(fields: [caseEntryId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  type     EvidenceType
  fileName String?
  fileUrl  String?
  mimeType String?
  size     Int?
  note     String?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([caseEntryId])
}

model ExportTemplate {
  id          String  @id @default(cuid())
  serviceSlug String?
  name        String
  content     String
  isDefault   Boolean @default(false)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([serviceSlug])
}

model Plan {
  id           String  @id @default(cuid())
  name         String
  slug         String  @unique
  priceMonthly Int     @default(0)
  priceYearly  Int     @default(0)
  isActive     Boolean @default(true)

  features      PlanFeature[]
  subscriptions Subscription[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt
}

model PlanFeature {
  id     String @id @default(cuid())
  planId String
  plan   Plan   @relation(fields: [planId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  key   String
  label String
  value String?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([planId])
  @@index([key])
}

model Subscription {
  id              String        @id @default(cuid())
  schoolAccountId String        @unique
  schoolAccount   SchoolAccount @relation(fields: [schoolAccountId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  planId String
  plan   Plan   @relation(fields: [planId], references: [id])

  status   SubscriptionStatus @default(TRIAL)
  startsAt DateTime           @default(now())
  endsAt   DateTime?

  transactions PaymentTransaction[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt
}

model ServiceAccess {
  id              String        @id @default(cuid())
  schoolAccountId String
  schoolAccount   SchoolAccount @relation(fields: [schoolAccountId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  serviceId String
  service   Service @relation(fields: [serviceId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  isEnabled Boolean @default(true)
  isPaid    Boolean @default(false)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([schoolAccountId, serviceId])
}

model FeatureFlag {
  id        String  @id @default(cuid())
  key       String  @unique
  label     String
  isEnabled Boolean @default(false)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt
}

model UsageLimit {
  id         String @id @default(cuid())
  planSlug   String
  featureKey String
  limit      Int

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@unique([planSlug, featureKey])
}

model UsageRecord {
  id              String @id @default(cuid())
  schoolAccountId String
  featureKey      String
  amount          Int    @default(1)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([schoolAccountId])
  @@index([featureKey])
}

model PaymentProvider {
  id         String  @id @default(cuid())
  name       String
  slug       String  @unique
  isActive   Boolean @default(false)
  configJson Json?

  transactions PaymentTransaction[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt
}

model PaymentTransaction {
  id             String        @id @default(cuid())
  subscriptionId String?
  subscription   Subscription? @relation(fields: [subscriptionId], references: [id])

  providerId String?
  provider   PaymentProvider? @relation(fields: [providerId], references: [id])

  amount       Int
  currency     String        @default("SAR")
  method       PaymentMethod
  status       PaymentStatus @default(PENDING)
  externalRef  String?
  metadataJson Json?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([subscriptionId])
  @@index([providerId])
  @@index([status])
}

model BankTransferRequest {
  id              String        @id @default(cuid())
  schoolAccountId String
  amount          Int
  currency        String        @default("SAR")
  senderName      String?
  receiptUrl      String?
  status          PaymentStatus @default(PENDING)
  adminNote       String?

  planId          String?
  durationDays    Int?
  requesterUserId String?
  billingCycle    String?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([schoolAccountId])
  @@index([status])
}

model ManualActivation {
  id              String    @id @default(cuid())
  schoolAccountId String
  activatedById   String?
  reason          String?
  startsAt        DateTime  @default(now())
  endsAt          DateTime?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([schoolAccountId])
}

model IntegrationProvider {
  id     String            @id @default(cuid())
  name   String
  slug   String            @unique
  status IntegrationStatus @default(INACTIVE)

  credentials ApiCredential[]
  webhooks    WebhookEvent[]
  syncLogs    ExternalSyncLog[]

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt
}

model ApiCredential {
  id         String              @id @default(cuid())
  providerId String
  provider   IntegrationProvider @relation(fields: [providerId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  label          String
  keyName        String
  encryptedValue String

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([providerId])
}

model WebhookEvent {
  id         String               @id @default(cuid())
  providerId String?
  provider   IntegrationProvider? @relation(fields: [providerId], references: [id])

  eventType   String
  payloadJson Json?
  processed   Boolean   @default(false)
  processedAt DateTime?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([providerId])
  @@index([processed])
}

model ExternalSyncLog {
  id         String               @id @default(cuid())
  providerId String?
  provider   IntegrationProvider? @relation(fields: [providerId], references: [id])

  entityType String
  entityId   String?
  status     String
  message    String?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([providerId])
  @@index([entityType])
}

model ResultsAnalysis {
  id              String         @id @default(cuid())
  schoolAccountId String?
  schoolAccount   SchoolAccount? @relation(fields: [schoolAccountId], references: [id])

  title      String
  grade      String?
  classroom  String?
  sourceFile String?
  status     String  @default("COMPLETED")

  totalStudents Int    @default(0)
  totalSubjects Int    @default(0)
  averageScore  Float?

  summaryJson Json?
  rowsJson    Json?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([schoolAccountId])
  @@index([grade])
  @@index([classroom])
}

enum ReportStatus {
  DRAFT
  GENERATED
  APPROVED
  ARCHIVED
}

enum ReportTemplateType {
  SYSTEM
  SCHOOL
  PERSONAL
}

model GuidanceReport {
  id          String @id @default(cuid())
  title       String
  serviceSlug String

  caseEntryId String
  caseEntry   CaseEntry @relation(fields: [caseEntryId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  status ReportStatus @default(DRAFT)

  genderMode String @default("MALE")

  editableContent String
  renderedContent String?

  evidenceEnabled Boolean @default(true)

  templateId         String?
  templateSnapshot   Json?
  reportDataSnapshot Json?
  generatedAt        DateTime?
  generatedPdfUrl    String?

  approvedAt DateTime?
  archivedAt DateTime?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  evidenceItems ReportEvidence[]

  @@index([caseEntryId])
  @@index([serviceSlug])
  @@index([status])
  @@index([templateId])
  @@index([generatedAt])
}

model ReportEvidence {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  reportId String
  report   GuidanceReport @relation(fields: [reportId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  fileName String
  fileUrl  String
  caption  String?

  mimeType String?
  size     Int?

  sortOrder Int     @default(0)
  visible   Boolean @default(true)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([reportId])
  @@index([visible])
}

model ReportTemplate {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  name        String
  description String?

  serviceSlug String?

  type ReportTemplateType @default(SYSTEM)

  content String

  templateJson Json?
  version      Int   @default(1)

  genderAware Boolean @default(true)

  usageCount Int     @default(0)
  isActive   Boolean @default(true)

  createdById String?
  createdBy   User?   @relation(fields: [createdById], references: [id])

  createdAt   DateTime   @default(now())
  updatedAt   DateTime   @updatedAt
  caseEntry   CaseEntry? @relation(fields: [caseEntryId], references: [id])
  caseEntryId String?

  @@index([serviceSlug])
  @@index([type])
  @@index([isActive])
  @@index([createdById])
}

model StudentImportSession {
  id              String        @id @default(cuid())
  schoolAccountId String
  schoolAccount   SchoolAccount @relation(fields: [schoolAccountId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  title  String
  source String              @default("NOOR_EXCEL")
  status ImportSessionStatus @default(DRAFT)

  totalRows     Int @default(0)
  validRows     Int @default(0)
  invalidRows   Int @default(0)
  createdCount  Int @default(0)
  updatedCount  Int @default(0)
  skippedCount  Int @default(0)
  conflictCount Int @default(0)

  files StudentImportFile[]
  rows  StudentImportRow[]

  committedAt DateTime?
  createdAt   DateTime  @default(now())
  updatedAt   DateTime  @updatedAt

  @@index([schoolAccountId])
  @@index([status])
}

model StudentImportFile {
  id        String               @id @default(cuid())
  sessionId String
  session   StudentImportSession @relation(fields: [sessionId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  fileName String
  mimeType String?
  size     Int?
  rowCount Int     @default(0)

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([sessionId])
}

model StudentImportRow {
  id        String               @id @default(cuid())
  sessionId String
  session   StudentImportSession @relation(fields: [sessionId], references: [id], onDelete: Cascade)

  rowIndex Int
  status   ImportRowStatus @default(PENDING)

  fullName      String
  nationalId    String?
  gender        Gender  @default(UNKNOWN)
  stage         String?
  grade         String?
  classroom     String?
  guardianName  String?
  guardianPhone String?

  matchedStudentId String?
  errorMessage     String?
  rawJson          Json?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([sessionId])
  @@index([nationalId])
  @@index([status])
}

model CaseEvidence {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  caseEntryId String?
  caseEntry   CaseEntry? @relation(fields: [caseEntryId], references: [id])

  fileName String
  fileUrl  String
  mimeType String
  size     Int

  uploadedById String?
  uploadedBy   User?   @relation(fields: [uploadedById], references: [id])

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([caseEntryId])
}

model ActivationCode {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  code String @unique

  label        String?
  durationDays Int     @default(30)

  maxUses   Int @default(1)
  usedCount Int @default(0)

  isActive Boolean @default(true)

  schoolAccountId String?
  createdById     String?
  usedByUserId    String?

  startsAt   DateTime?
  expiresAt  DateTime?
  lastUsedAt DateTime?

  note String?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())
  updatedAt DateTime @updatedAt

  @@index([code])
  @@index([isActive])
  @@index([schoolAccountId])
  @@index([createdById])
  @@index([usedByUserId])
}

model PlatformActivityLog {
  id String @id @default(cuid())

  actorUserId     String?
  targetUserId    String?
  schoolAccountId String?

  category String
  action   String
  severity String @default("INFO")

  title   String
  details Json?

  ipAddress String?
  userAgent String?

  createdAt DateTime @default(now())

  @@index([actorUserId])
  @@index([targetUserId])
  @@index([schoolAccountId])
  @@index([category])
  @@index([action])
  @@index([createdAt])
}
